In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circulars Nos. 21 of 1869, 14 of 1872, and 6 of 1875, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarter of the year 1899. b. The Custom House ( 01 ) was opened on the ist July I899. The collection of Tariff Import and Export Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.2h 30 + 8o per picul), and the collection of Native Duties and Likin on Junk Cargoes at half Tariff rates, commenced on&#39;that day. The stations at present provisionally controlled by the Kiaochow Customs are known as Taputou ( :* A); Lingshanwei (3 i), including Tachiangkou ( ); Nuku (%c fd); Tsangkou (# 1); and Shatszkou (SP J ), including Tengyao ( ). c. The word &quot;Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China, the statistics of goods landed and consumed in German territory not being included. I. PARTI V. NOTIFICATIONS, During the Quarter ended December 31, 1899.
208 cusToMs GAZTT. [ocT.-DEC., 1899.1 NOTICES TO MARINERS. CHINA SEA. No. 347. SWATOW DISTRICT. NEWLY DISCOVERED ROCKS TO THE SOUTHWARD OF NAMOA ISLAND. NOTICE is hereby given of the existence of two newly discovered rocks to the southward of Namoa Island, having I8 feet of water over them at low water of spring tides. To these the names of Tamsui Rock and Kaipan Rock have been given. From Tamsui Rock the Pagoda on Namoa, Island bears N. 410 W., and South Point, Namoa Island, bears N. 60 E. From Kaipan Rock the Pagoda on Namoa Island bears N. 450 W., and South Point, Namoa Island, bears N. 78 E SEHA.1U, 21st October 189o.

APPENDIX I. KOWLOON AND LAPPA: QUARTERLY RETURNS OF TRADE. OOTOBER-DEOEMBER 1899* 29
2(6 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. [OCT.-DEC., (Received 16th January 1goo.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, IITn JANUARY 1900. SIR,I I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the fourth quarter of the year I899, October-December. b. The Custom House (A( &pound; N&amp;gt; was opened on the 2nd April 1887, and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, began on that day. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Hk.M 3o and Hk.Mle 8o per picul) commenced on the 14th April 1887, and the collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, on the ist July 1887. The stations then controlled by the Kowloon Customs were known as Capsuimoon (t *) &#39;), Changchow (R f), Fotochow (A Ai N), and Kowloon (At UE). On the 5th October 1899, as a result of the extension of territory to form part of the Colony of Hongkong, they were removed to Taishan (* Jjj) and Lintin (je3 ), on the north and west; to Samun (2 Pf), on the east; and to Shaiichung (1A. f ), on the Mirs Bay littoral. The Custom House is now called . r. The word &quot;Inwards &quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II. 236 CUSTOMS GAErTTr. - T-i- (Received 29th January i9oo.) CUSTOM HOUSE, LAPPA, 24TH JANUARY 1900. SIR, L-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade of this port for the fourth quarter, of the year I899, October-Deceiber. * b. The Custom House ( ) was opened on the 2nd April 1887. The collection of Tariff Import Duty and Convention Likin on Opium (Bk.T 3o and Hk.h 8o per picul) and the collection of Provincial Likin and Ching-fei Tax on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Provincial Tariff received from the Viceroy of the Two Kwang, com- *menced on that day. The collection of Native Duties on General Cargo, at the rates fixed by the Native Custom House Tariff received from the Hoppo, commenced on the ist July 1887. The stations at present controlled by the Lappa Customs are known as the Malowchow ( &#39; ), Tungho (3 ( ), and Chienshan (fi th) stations, with three sub-stations, known as the Shekkok (; 6 ), Kuttai (W 1C), and Kwanchiap ( ) sub-stations. c. The word Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot;Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
